### [68] [Neutrino and electromagnetic signatures from Superluminous Supernovae: a case study for SN 2017egm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24655)

Result: 对于最近观测到的SLSNe SN 2017egm，模型预测的高能伽马射线与Fermi LAT的最近探测结果相符。对于微中子，以SN 2017egm作为典型SLSNe，发现在Vera C. Rubin Observatory时代，通过堆积分析，未来微中子观测站在十年运行期内可实现对SLSNe群体微中子事件的3σ显著性探测。

Conclusion: 毫秒磁星驱动模型能够解释SLSNe的观测特征，特别是高能伽马射线信号。多信使观测（特别是微中子）为验证这一理论提供了重要途径，未来观测设施有望在统计显著性上确认SLSNe的磁星起源。

Abstract: Superluminous supernovae (SLSNe) are rare transients that are $\sim 10 - 100$ times more luminous than ordinary stellar explosions, reaching peak optical luminosities $\sim 10^{44} - 10^{45}$ erg s$^{-1}$. The energy source powering SLSNe remains uncertain. In this work, we explore the multi-wavelength and multi-messenger signatures of the scenario in which SLSNe are powered by a newly born millisecond magnetar. We model the dynamical evolution and emission from the coupled system comprised of the magnetar, wind, nebula, and supernova ejecta, consistently evaluating the pair multiplicity of the wind and nebula regions, and the bulk wind Lorentz factor governing the $e^+ - e^-$ injection spectra in the nebula. We compute the thermal and non-thermal electromagnetic signatures, neutrino signatures, and investigate their detection prospects. For SN 2017egm, the nearest observed SLSNe, our prediction for high-energy gamma rays matches the recent detection by Fermi LAT. For neutrinos, using SN 2017egm a canonical SLSNe, we find that in the era of the Vera C. Rubin Observatory, a stacking analysis with upcoming neutrino observatories can lead to $3σ$ detection significance of neutrino events from a population of SLSNe within a decade of operation.

</details>

*Tullia Sbarrato,Silvia Belladitta,Julien Wolf,Pietro Baldini,Dusan Tubín-Arenas,Mara Salvato,Emmanuel Momjian,Steven Hämmerich,Andrea Merloni,Werner Collmar,Joern Wilms*

TL;DR: BLAZ4R是首个z>4的确认耀变体活体目录，重点关注eROSITA的贡献，包含54个确认耀变体，用于研究早期宇宙中相对论性喷流的形成和演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用eROSITA（SRG航天器上的仪器）的全天巡天数据，结合X射线特性、射电光谱和形态学以及多波段轮廓分析，构建了包含54个确认耀变体的BLAZ4R活体目录。通过X射线探测、光谱和时间研究来表征这些喷流源的特性。

Result: 确认喷流源在高红移处相对于非喷流对应物数量显著更多；耀变体（以及整体喷流种群）在早期宇宙中与总活动星系核种群相比没有显著不同的特征；需要快速吸积过程来解释z>4处完全形成的喷流活动星系核的存在。

Conclusion: BLAZ4R目录为研究早期宇宙喷流活动星系核的形成提供了重要工具。虽然需要快速吸积过程来解释这些喷流源的存在，但目前的多波段特性多样性尚不能识别这些过程的具体特征。未来将继续更新BLAZ4R以寻找这些特征，最终理解喷流活动星系核的早期形成机制。

Abstract: We present BLAZ4R, the first living catalog of confirmed $z>4$ blazars, with a focus on the contribution of eROSITA, on board of the Spectrum Roentgen Gamma (SRG) spacecraft. Blazars at $z>4$ are rare but powerful probes of how active supermassive black holes evolve in connection to relativistic jets, in the first 2 billion years of cosmic history. At these redshifts, X-ray observations are essential for constraining blazars jet power and orientation, enabling effective trace of their parent population. The all-sky surveys provided by eROSITA ensure X-ray detection for BLAZ4R sources and, in some cases, allow spectral and temporal studies of their jetted emission. BLAZ4R includes 54 confirmed blazars, characterized through their X-ray properties, radio spectra and morphology, and multiwavelength profiles. We confirm that jetted sources are significantly more numerous relative to non-jetted counterparts at high-$z$, and that blazars (and therefore the overall jetted population) do not exhibit significantly different features compared to the total active galactic nuclei population in the early Universe. Fast accretion processes that involve relativistic jets are clearly required to justify the existence of fully formed jetted AGN at $z>4$. However, the diverse multiwavelength properties characterizing BLAZ4R do not yet allow us to identify the specific signatures of these processes. We will continue updating BLAZ4R to search for such signatures and ultimately understand the early formation of jetted AGN.

</details>

*Madison Reich,W. N. Brandt,Bin Luo,Richard Plotkin,Ohad Shemmer,Fabio Vito,Weimin Yi*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 使用钱德拉望远镜对10个弱线类星体进行多周期监测，每个目标获得3次2.8-8.2 ks的新观测，结合历史数据，分析X射线通量变化，并与普通射电宁静类星体进行统计比较。

Result: 在SDSS J1539+3954中观察到反复的极端X射线变异性（2023-2024年间通量上升≥6倍）；SDSS J0825+1155在2019-2023年间通量变化≥14倍；约20%的WLQs显示极端X射线变异性；WLQs出现极端变异性事件的几率比普通射电宁静类星体高约6.8倍。

Conclusion: 弱线类星体表现出比普通射电宁静类星体更高的极端X射线变异性概率，这可能与厚盘和外流模型中的内禀风运动有关，而非盘高度的变化。

Abstract: We present the results of a multi-cycle Chandra program to systematically monitor the X-ray variability of 10 weak-line quasars (WLQs) that previously had limited multi-epoch X-ray observations. Three new Chandra 2.8 to 8.2 ks observations were obtained for each WLQ with C$\,$IV rest-frame equivalent widths (REWs) $\lesssim 10$ Å, substantially improving the monitoring data quality of WLQs and our ability to characterize their long-term X-ray variability behavior. We observe recurrent extreme X-ray variability in the historically variable WLQ SDSS J1539+3954, with an X-ray flux rise of a factor of $\gtrsim 6$ between 2023 and 2024 ($\gtrsim 21$ relative to 2013). Another previously X-ray weak WLQ in the sample, SDSS J0825+1155, underwent a significant X-ray flux variation by a factor of $\gtrsim 14$ between 2019 and 2023. We find the fraction of WLQs exhibiting evidence of extreme X-ray variability to be $0.20^{+0.17}_{-0.07}$. In the context of the thick disk and outflow (TDO) model, the substantial fraction of WLQs displaying extreme X-ray variability may suggest that the variability is driven by the intrinsic motion of the TDO wind rather than changes in the height of the TDO disk. We performed a statistical comparison between the distribution of variability amplitudes of WLQs and general radio-quiet quasars. We find that these underlying distributions are statistically different, with WLQs having $\approx 6.8$ times higher odds of exhibiting an extreme X-ray variability event than the general radio-quiet quasar population.

</details>

Result: 激波产生有界吸积爆发，形成谐波级联的功率谱密度；吸积流叠加产生谱拍频，可精确提取双星质量比；激波加速电子的辐射冷却产生从同步射电连续谱到逆康普顿伽马射线尾的多波段谱能分布；识别出高频引力波边带"背景气体嗡鸣"，表现为离散高频爆发序列，其时间静默间隙随腔体收缩而系统压缩；腔体内活跃气体质量在解耦前剧烈放大，在4.0 mHz附近产生终端爆发，作为最终真空螺旋的多信使前兆。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了停滞超大质量黑洞双星周期性质量转移的完整多信使理论框架，揭示了吸积爆发、多波段辐射和引力波信号的物理联系，特别是识别出的高频引力波边带信号和终端爆发为探测双星演化晚期阶段提供了关键观测特征。

Abstract: We establish the multi-messenger mechanics of episodic mass transfer in supermassive black hole binaries stalled within circumbinary discs. Utilizing continuous wavelet transforms, we isolate localized gas clumps at the cavity edge and track their evolution. By regularizing the forced fluid equations at Lindblad resonances via the inhomogeneous Airy differential equation, we bypass linear singularities to extract the finite wave amplitudes that trigger non-linear shock formation. These shocks produce bounded accretion bursts. We model the time-domain thermal luminosity, deriving an analytical power spectral density that forms a harmonic cascade. The superposition of the accretion streams generates a spectral beat frequency, providing an exact mathematical extraction of the binary mass ratio. The radiative cooling of shock-accelerated electrons produces a multi-wavelength spectral energy distribution from a synchrotron radio continuum to an inverse-Compton gamma-ray tail. We identify a relativistic signature: a discontinuous, high-frequency gravitational wave sideband termed the ``background gas humming''. This emission arises from the highly asymmetric, transient fluid geometry of the accretion shocks. Evaluating the asymptotic properties of the Airy regularization, we show that this humming manifests as a sequence of discrete high-frequency bursts with temporal quiescence gaps that systematically compress as the cavity shrinks. We show that the instantaneous mass of the gas actively trapped within the cavity violently amplifies prior to decoupling, culminating in a terminal burst near 4.0 mHz that serves as a multi-messenger precursor to the final vacuum inspiral.

</details>

Abstract: Black hole X-ray binaries (BHXRBs) have traditionally been discovered by X-ray surveys with cadences of hours to days. However, large optical time-domain surveys now provide novel avenues for early detection and insights into their elusive outburst triggering mechanisms. We present early-time light curves of the BHXRB AT 2019wey serendipitously observed by the Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS). The TESS images are sampled at 30 minute cadence from $\approx2$ d prior to $\approx25$ d after outburst, providing the highest time resolution optical rising phase observations of any known BHXRB. We fit a piece-wise power law to the rising light curve, finding an outburst onset time of MJD $58817.86\pm0.09$ and power-law rise index $n=0.74\pm0.04$. The onset time precedes all ground-based optical detections, and suggests that the optical rise began after the start of the faint X-ray brightening in MAXI data. We search for periodic high frequency modulation and detect none exceeding amplitude $\approx0.48$ mJy at periods of $\gtrsim1$ h at 90% confidence.

### [73] [Photon Ring Astrometry I: A Simple Spin Measurement Technique for High-Resolution Images of M87*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24722)
*Delilah E. A. Gates,Dominic O. Chang,Aaron Held,Daniel C. M. Palumbo*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 提出利用M87黑洞光子环亚像与直接像中心位移进行自旋测量的新方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对M87中心超大质量黑洞的自旋测量需要高分辨率视界尺度成像，但现有方法依赖对观测发射的几何建模。本文旨在开发一种不依赖几何建模的自旋约束技术。

Method: 提出利用n=1光子环亚像与直接像中心的相对位移作为可观测物理量。假设M87大尺度喷流与黑洞自旋轴对齐，将光子环相对位置分解为平行和垂直于投影自旋轴的分量，并用n=1亚像直径归一化。平行位移主要取决于倾角和发射半径，横向位移与倾角和自旋紧密相关。

Abstract: The central supermassive black hole of the galaxy M87 is currently a target for precision spin measurement using high-resolution, horizon-scale imaging. Such observations aim to resolve the first lensed (${n}~{=}~{1}$) sub-image of the photon ring from the broader direct image. In this work, we identify a concrete observable -- the displacement between the centers of the ${n}~{=}~{1}$ photon-ring sub-image and the direct image -- and propose its use in a simple spin-measurement technique. Leveraging the assumption that the observed large-scale jet of M87 is aligned with the black-hole spin axis, we separate the relative position of the photon ring into components parallel and transverse to the projected spin axis, normalizing both components with respect to the measured diameter of the ${n}~{=}~{1}$ sub-image. We show that the parallel shift is primarily determined by inclination and emission radius, while the transverse shift is tightly correlated with inclination and spin. We demonstrate these effects both in a simple geometric model (to explain the underlying physics) and in GRMHD simulations with magnetically arrested disks (to provide realistic instantiations of the effect). We find that a relative astrometric resolution of ${\lesssim}~{0.1\;μ\rm{as}}$ is sufficient to constrain the spin to better than 9% if the accretion flow is prograde or 22% if the flow is retrograde. If the direction of the accretion flow is undetermined, the spin can be constrained to within 26%. More generally, this identifies relative photon ring astrometry as a promising method to constrain the underlying spacetime geometry and introduces a spin-constraint technique that does not rely on geometric modeling of the observed emission.

Abstract: Diffuse gamma-ray emission is a key tracer of cosmic rays (CRs) in galaxies, encoding information about their transport, energetics, and interactions with the interstellar medium. Interpreting the Milky Way gamma-ray sky is challenging because the observed emission depends on the three-dimensional distributions of CRs and gas, as well as the observer location within the Galaxy. Using the Rhea suite of CR-MHD simulations of a Milky Way analogue, we study how pion-decay gamma-ray emission varies with galactic environment, local conditions, and CR transport physics. Emission is computed in post-processing under steady-state assumptions, enabling analysis of luminosities, spectra, full-sky maps, and angular power spectra (APS) for multiple observer positions, including those inside Local-Bubble-like cavities. The simulated galaxy reproduces Milky Way-like gamma-ray luminosities and spectral slopes without parameter tuning. While total luminosity is relatively stable, the morphology of the gamma-ray sky varies strongly with observer location due to the complex local gas distribution, consistent with observations. For all observers, the APS traces gas column density rather than the smoother CR energy density, in agreement with previous studies. Comparisons with Fermi-LAT data show good agreement in both the all-sky spectrum and APS, with a diffusion coefficient scaling proportional to E^0.5 providing the best match. These results show that key features of Galactic gamma-ray emission arise naturally in self-consistent CR-MHD simulations. Gas density fluctuations primarily shape emission morphology, while CR transport governs spectral and structural properties. The Rhea simulations provide a physically grounded framework for interpreting diffuse gamma-ray observations and highlight the importance of local environment in tracing Galactic CR physics.

Abstract: The merger between the carbon (C) and oxygen (O) shells hours to days before the collapse of a massive star significantly changes its nucleosynthesis, which is reflected in the elemental ratios observed in supernova remnants (SNRs). We present a nucleosynthesis study of $^{44}$Ti production in core-collapse supernovae (CCSNe), highlighting large silicon (Si), sulfur (S), calcium (Ca), and, most importantly, argon (Ar) to neon (Ne) ratios as diagnostics for carbon-oxygen (C--O) shell mergers. We compare yields from eight different sets of CCSNe models to observations of Cassiopeia A (Cas A), and show that C--O shell mergers are consistently the models that best match X-ray and infrared observations. These models produce high Ar/Ne ratios ($\gtrsim 0.1$), due to $^{20}$Ne depletion and production of $^{36}$Ar and $^{38}$Ar, while lower ratios are obtained from non-merger cases. Based on the Ar/Ne diagnostic, we compare the range of expected $^{44}$Ti produced by C--O shell mergers, which is up to $\sim 20 - 30 \%$ of the overall $^{44}$Ti, but expected to be located outside the reverse shock. Based on the sets of models considered, the photon flux expected from the $^{44}$Ti synthesized in the C--O shell merger in Cas A is below the $NuSTAR$ and $COSI$ detection limits, compatible with current limits locating most of the $^{44}$Ti interior to the reverse shock, but might be detectable from proposed missions like $ASCENT$. Finally, for the SNR of 1987A, a dominant C--O merger origin of the observed $^{44}$Ti is unlikely based on the observed redshift in its $^{44}$Ti line.

Abstract: {Supernova (SN) siblings are powerful tools used to calibrate and improve distance measurement methods, and to make the systematic uncertainty to distances to their host galaxies considerably lower compared to other techniques.}
  {In this paper we present distance estimates to NGC6951, a galaxy that hosted the Type IIP SN~2020dpw, the Type Ib SN~2021sjt, and three other SNe.}
  {Photometric observations of the two objects were carried out using two 80cm RC telescopes located in Hungary, while spectra were obtained from the LCO and the WiseRep database. For the distance estimates, we applied the expanding photosphere method (EPM), which connects the observed angular radius ($θ$) of a SN to its physical radius and is related to the velocity of the photosphere ($v_{\rm ph}$). Although the EPM is mostly applied to derive the distance of Type IIP SNe, in the literature there are several examples of this technique being used for Type IIn and stripped-envelope SNe as well. Therefore, we made another attempt to infer the distance of the Type Ib SN~2021sjt by applying the EPM together with its Type IIP sibling SN~2020dpw. }
  {Our analysis resulted in a distance of $25.76 \pm 0.34 (\rm random) \pm 5.51$ (systematic) Mpc and $24.57 \pm 1.27 (\rm random) \pm 4.64$ (systematic) Mpc for SN~2020dpw and SN~2021sjt, respectively. Systematic errors were estimated with respect to the used dilution factor, the interstellar reddening, and the date of the explosion (which was fixed to a value between the last non-detection and the first detection for each object).}
  {The obtained distance values agree with each other and with the literature, which shows the validity of the methods used. In this way, new and perhaps improved distance estimates to NGC 6951 were obtained, and the applicability of the EPM for Type Ib SNe was tested.}

Abstract: This study investigates the X-ray characteristics of jet-associated radio-quiet AGNs across distinct optical/UV to X-ray correlation phases. Quasi-simultaneous optical/UV/X-ray observations of NGC 4051 from May-June 2009, obtained through Swift and XMM-Newton, reveal a temporal dichotomy: a strong optical/UV to X-ray correlation dominates the initial observation phase (before May 27), followed by an optical/UV flare event concurrent with X-ray flux suppression in the latter period. Our multi-method analysis of XMM-Newton data, incorporating short-term X-ray variability assessment, spectral decomposition, and RGS spectral analysis, identifies significant inter-phase X-ray emission disparities. During optical/UV flaring episodes, compared to the correlated phase, we observe: attenuated short-term X-ray variability amplitudes, enhanced soft X-ray absorption, suppressed intrinsic hard X-ray flux, and more prominent RGS emission-line features. Notably, these X-ray characteristics during optical/UV flaring intervals show no statistically significant deviations from pre-flare low-state X-ray emission patterns. These non-synchronous optical/UV-X-ray variations contradict predictions from both reprocessing models, starburst-driven emission scenarios, and the simplistic absorption models. While potential jet-related mechanisms remain ambiguous, our findings demonstrate strong consistency with predictions from the inhomogeneous accretion disk perturbation framework.

Abstract: We present a search for neutrino counterparts to fast radio bursts (FRBs) using temporal and spatial cross-matching between the Second CHIME/FRB catalogue and the IceCube high-energy alert-track catalogue ICECAT1. Because current FRB--neutrino models do not provide a unique consensus on emission ordering, our primary significance test adopts a two-sided, order-agnostic temporal hypothesis. The analysis accounts for declination-dependent CHIME/FRB exposure and the look-elsewhere effect across multiple trials. No statistically significant FRB--neutrino association is found. The most significant pair is FRB\,20190630C--IC\,190629A, with a post-trial probability of $p=0.076$ ($1.43σ$), consistent with a chance coincidence. Within our statistical framework, a detectable physical association would require a time offset shorter than $\sim256$~s at $3σ$ or $\sim63$~ms at $5σ$. Using a population-level stacking analysis, we derive 90\% upper limits on the neutrino-to-radio luminosity ratio of FRBs, $ξ\lesssim 10^{8}-10^{11}$ for neutrino power-law spectral indices $γ=1.0-3.0$. These limits improve upon previous constraints by approximately two orders of magnitude and represent the most stringent bounds from FRB--neutrino coincidence searches to date. Although the current limits remain above the predictions of most magnetar-based models, they begin to constrain scenarios involving exceptionally efficient hadronic energy dissipation.

Abstract: AstroSat/LAXPC and SXT observed the persistent neutron star low-mass X-ray binary 4U 1820-30 between 2016 and 2022. During these observations, the hardness-intensity diagram (HID) and color-color diagram (CCD) indicated that the source was in the banana state. We divided the CCD into 11 segments for spectral and timing analyses. For each segment in the CCD, we modeled the spectral data using two distinct approaches over the 0.7-20.0 keV band. A combination of a multi-color-disk component with an inner disk temperature of around 0.6 keV and Comptonized emission from the boundary layer (BL)/ hot corona provided the best description of the X-ray spectral data of this source. The truncation radius was found to be in the range of $\sim$ 19-40 km. The Comptonized component has an optical depth in the range of $\sim 7 - 13$ with electron temperature in the range of $\sim 2.5 - 3.8$ keV. The optical depth of the corona varies significantly along the position on the CCD, while $\sim$ 80\% of the X-ray flux comes from the Comptonized component. We discuss possible physical scenarios to explain the relationship between the spectral evolution and motion of the source along the CCD. The timing analysis revealed kHz QPOs peaks at $\sim 710$ Hz and $\sim 740$ Hz in the lower left banana branch. An energy-dependent study indicates that these QPOs are stronger in the high-energy band.

</details>


### [80] [The 'Forgotten' Neutrons: Implications for the Propagation of High-Energy Cosmic Rays in Magnetized Astrophysical and Cosmological Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25060)
*Ellis R. Owen,Kinwah Wu,Yoshiyuki Inoue,Tatsuki Fujiwara,Qin Han,Hayden P. H. Ng*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 宇宙线在中子介导的传播中实现跨结构转移，改变了传统磁约束模型下的逃逸机制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统宇宙线传播模型假设粒子保持带电状态并被磁场约束，但高能强子相互作用可将质子转化为中子，中子不受磁场影响可进行弹道传播，这种带电-中性转换机制对宇宙线在不同宇宙结构间的交换影响尚未被充分研究

Method: 采用扩散/流传播方法求解四种代表性环境（银河系、星暴星系、星系团、宇宙学纤维）中受限宇宙线质子的输运，结合强子pp和pγ相互作用，将中子产生和衰变处理为随机泊松"跳跃"过程

Result: 中子介导的传播步骤允许宇宙线从大尺度宇宙结构中额外逃逸，特别是在传统带电粒子输运预测强约束和辐射场衰减的能量区域；考虑中子传播后，超高能宇宙线从嵌入源向纤维和空洞的转移方式发生质变

Conclusion: 带电-中性转换机制显著改变宇宙线在宇宙大尺度结构中的约束和逃逸行为，对宇宙线在宇宙结构间的分配产生重要影响，需要重新评估传统磁约束传播模型

Abstract: Cosmological filaments, galaxy clusters, and galaxies are magnetized reservoirs of cosmic rays (CRs). The exchange of CRs across these structures is usually modeled assuming that they remain charged and magnetically confined. At high energies, hadronic interactions can convert CR protons to neutrons. This physics is routinely included in air-shower and ultra-high-energy (UHE) CR propagation Monte Carlo simulations used for composition studies but is rarely treated explicitly in propagation models of CR transport and exchange between magnetized reservoirs. CR neutrons are not affected by magnetic fields and can propagate ballistically over kpc-Mpc distances before decaying back into protons, with relativistic time dilation extending their effective decay length. We show how such charged-neutral switching modifies CR confinement and escape in four representative environments: a Milky Way-like galaxy, a starburst galaxy, a galaxy cluster, and a cosmological filament. By solving the transport of a confined CR proton population in each structure using a diffusion/streaming propagation approach with hadronic pp and p$γ$ interactions, and treating neutron production and decay as a stochastic Poisson ''jump'' process, we find that neutron-mediated steps can allow additional CR escape from large-scale cosmological structures at energies where charged-particle transport alone would predict strong CR confinement and attenuation in ambient radiation fields. These effects imply a qualitative shift in how ultra-high-energy CRs are transferred from embedded sources into filaments and voids once intermediate neutron propagation is considered, with consequences for the partitioning of CRs across the large-scale structure of the Universe.

</details>


### [81] [Binary neutron star mergers with a subsolar mass star](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25102)
*Maxence Corman,William E. East,Jocelyn S. Read*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 研究亚太阳质量致密天体（0.8 M☉中子星）与正常质量中子星（1.7 M☉）并合的数值模拟，分析其引力波和电磁信号特征，评估当前探测器对这些系统的探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚太阳质量致密天体（如原初黑洞或坍缩盘形成的中子星）的存在尚未被观测证实。如果这类天体存在，双星并合的引力波可能揭示它们。研究亚太阳质量中子星与正常质量中子星的并合，旨在确定当前模型对这些系统的描述能力，以及如何区分亚太阳质量黑洞系统。

Method: 采用完全广义相对论数值模拟，研究1.7 M☉中子星与0.8 M☉中子星的并合过程。量化亚太阳质量中子星高达O(10^4)的潮汐形变参数对并合动力学、引力波和电磁信号的影响。

Result: 发现质量转移现象和显著降低的瓦解频率，这些特征未被当前引力波波形模型捕捉。对于信噪比≲100的情况，LIGO和Virgo探测器在达到设计灵敏度时不会产生内在参数估计偏差。大形变参数导致动力学抛射物质显著增加，超过现有唯象模型的预测。

Conclusion: 亚太阳质量中子星并合系统的特征虽未被当前引力波模型完全描述，但不显著影响当前引力波探测器对这些源的探测灵敏度。大潮汐形变导致更多物质抛射，这对电磁对应体观测有重要意义。

Abstract: While there are a number of proposed formation channels for subsolar mass compact objects, including black holes formed primordially, or neutron stars that form in collapsar disks, there have yet to be any conclusive observations of such objects. Motivated by the possibility that, if such objects exist, gravitational waves from binary mergers may reveal them, we study binary neutron star mergers where one star has a subsolar-mass in order to determine how well such systems are described by current models, and when they could be distinguished from a system with a subsolar-mass black hole. We perform fully general-relativistic simulations of a $1.7\ M_{\odot}$ star merging with a $0.8\ M_{\odot}$ star, leading to tidal deformabilities of up to $\mathcal{O}(10^4)$ for the latter, and quantify how this affects the merger dynamics and associated gravitation and electromagnetic signals. In this regime, we find mass transfer between the stars, as well as significantly lower disruption frequencies. Though this is not captured by current gravitational waveform models, we conclude that this does not significantly impact the sensitivity of current gravitational wave detectors to these sources. Assuming design sensitivity of the LIGO and Virgo detectors, we find no biases in the recovered intrinsic parameters for signal-to-noise ratios $\lesssim 100$. We also find that the large deformabilities lead to a significant increase in the amount of dynamically ejected matter compared to equal mass systems, exceeding the predictions of current phenomenological models.

</details>


### [82] [Jet Power Estimates of FSRQs PKS 1441+25 and Ton 599 from Broadband SED Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25331)
*Hritwik Bora,Ranjeev Misra,Rukaiya Khatoon,Rupjyoti Gogoi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: FSRQ喷流功率估计对粒子能量分布模型不敏感，与HBLs形成对比


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究FSRQs喷流功率估计对粒子能量分布模型的依赖性，与HBLs的显著差异形成对比

Method: 对PKS 1441+25和Ton 599进行宽带SED建模，使用Swift-XRT/UVOT、NuSTAR、Fermi-LAT和VERITAS观测数据，比较四种粒子分布模型：断幂律、对数抛物线、扩散时间能量依赖模型和加速时间能量依赖模型

Result: 具有内在曲率的模型（对数抛物线和能量依赖模型）得到的喷流功率估计与断幂律分布模型处于同一数量级，这与HBLs中模型间功率估计可相差近两个数量级形成鲜明对比

Conclusion: FSRQs喷流功率估计相对不依赖于假设的粒子能量分布，这反映了外部康普顿过程的主导作用和较弱的光谱曲率依赖性

Abstract: Flat-Spectrum Radio Quasars (FSRQs) are among the most energetic and powerful active galactic nuclei, often exhibiting jet powers comparable to or exceeding the Eddington luminosity. In this work, we performed broadband spectral energy distribution (SED) modeling of two FSRQs PKS 1441+25 and Ton 599, using Swift-XRT/UVOT, NuSTAR, Fermi-LAT and VERITAS observations during 2015 and 2021, respectively. We considered four particle distribution models: a broken power law, a log-parabola, and two energy-dependent models in which either the diffusion or acceleration timescale depends on energy. Our results show that the jet power estimates derived from models with intrinsic curvature, such as the log-parabola and energy-dependent models, are of the same order as those obtained with a broken power-law distribution. This contrasts with the case of High Synchrotron Peaked Blazars (HBLs), where the power estimates can differ by nearly two orders of magnitude between models. We attribute this difference to the lower electron break energies typically observed in FSRQs. Consequently, our findings suggest that, unlike in HBLs, the estimated jet powers in FSRQs are relatively insensitive to the assumed particle energy distribution, reflecting the dominance of external Compton processes and weaker dependence on spectral curvature.

</details>


### [83] [Double-Adiabatic Equations of State for Relativistic Plasmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25669)
*Agnieszka Wierzchucka,Pablo J. Bilbao,Alexander G. R. Thomas,Dmitri A. Uzdensky,Alexander A. Schekochihin*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于系统对称性的第一性原理形式来推导绝热定律，恢复了各向同性等离子体的绝热状态方程和碰撞无磁化等离子体的双绝热状态方程，并将其扩展到相对论区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究等离子体绝热状态方程的推导方法，特别是为碰撞无磁化等离子体建立更普适的理论框架，并将其扩展到相对论区域，以描述复杂的高能天体物理过程。

Method: 提出基于系统对称性的第一性原理形式化方法，利用对称性推导绝热定律。该方法能够统一处理各向同性等离子体和碰撞无磁化等离子体，并扩展到相对论区域。

Result: 成功恢复了各向同性等离子体的绝热状态方程P∝n^Γ和碰撞无磁化等离子体的双绝热状态方程。在相对论区域发现双绝热状态方程的确切函数形式依赖于压力各向异性，不是简单的幂律关系。

Conclusion: 基于对称性的第一性原理方法为推导等离子体绝热定律提供了普适框架，扩展的双绝热状态方程能够描述从简单几何结构到复杂高能天体物理过程的广泛现象，包括磁重联中形成的等离子体团结构演化。

Abstract: The adiabatic equation of state $P \propto n^Γ$ describes the pressure evolution of highly collisional, isotropic plasmas in terms of their density, providing a possible closure of the fluid moment hierarchy in the absence of heat fluxes and dissipation. An analogous closure exists for collisionless, magnetised plasmas, whose pressure tensor is anisotropic with respect to the magnetic field, and the closure is therefore double-adiabatic, prescribing the evolution of the parallel and perpendicular pressures in terms of the magnetic-field strength and density. Here, we present a general first-principle formalism to derive adiabatic laws using the symmetries of the system. With this theory we recover the adiabatic equation of state $P \propto n^Γ$ for isotropic plasmas and the double-adiabatic equations of state for collisionless, magnetised plasmas. We extend the latter to the relativistic regime, finding that their exact functional form depends on the pressure anisotropy and is not a simple power law. Our double-adiabatic equations of state describe simple geometries, like magnetic mirrors or compressed homogeneous plasmas, as well as complex high-energy astrophysical processes, such as the evolution of plasmoid structures formed during magnetic reconnection.

</details>

### [84] [A capture-recapture hidden Markov model framework for register-based inference of population size and dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24643)
*Lucy Y Brown,Eleni Matechou,Bruno Santos,Eleonora Mussino*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出一个基于隐马尔可夫模型的捕获-再捕获框架，用于从存在误报和漏报错误的登记数据中推断人口规模和动态变化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人口普查成本高、频率低且不及时，许多国家采用登记系统作为补充或替代。但登记数据存在不完整性问题：个体可能被漏记（假阴性）或误记（假阳性）。现有方法要么忽略某些错误类型，要么只能推断静态人口规模而非动态变化，要么计算效率低下。

Method: 基于Cormack-Jolly-Seber类型的捕获-再捕获模型构建隐马尔可夫模型框架，使用最大似然估计进行推断，通过"小引导袋"方法量化不确定性。模型考虑临时迁出，纳入任意数量可能相互作用的登记系统，允许观测概率随个体特征和不可观测异质性变化。

Result: 使用瑞典人口登记数据进行应用，其中"过度覆盖"（已不在该国但仍在登记系统中）是一个典型案例。应用结果提供了关于人口动态和个体轨迹的新见解。

Conclusion: 提出了一个可扩展的框架，能够从存在观测误差的登记数据中准确推断人口规模和动态变化，为政策制定、资源分配和人口研究提供了更及时、成本效益更高的替代方案。

Abstract: Accurate inference on population dynamics, such as migration and changes in population size, is essential for policymaking, resource allocation and demographic research. Traditional censuses are expensive, infrequent and not timely, leading many countries to adopt register-based approaches to replace or complement them. A primary challenge is that such registers are incomplete: even when individuals are present, their activities may not generate records in specific registers, resulting in false negative observation error. Conversely, some registers arise from administrative or household-level processes, so that individuals may appear in registers despite being absent, leading to false positive observation error. Existing approaches often either rely on ad-hoc decisions that ignore one or both error types, offer inference on population snapshots but not dynamics, or are computationally too slow for practical use. We propose a scalable framework for inferring population size and dynamics from register data, building on Cormack-Jolly-Seber type capture-recapture models formulated as hidden Markov models. Inference is carried out using maximum likelihood estimation, with uncertainty quantified via the Bag of Little Bootstraps. The model accounts for temporary emigration, incorporates an arbitrary number of possibly interacting registers subject to both error types, and allows observation probabilities to vary with individual characteristics and unobservable heterogeneity. We illustrate the approach using Swedish population registers, where overcoverage - individuals registered as living in the country although they are no longer present - provides a motivating example. The application yields new insights into population dynamics and individual trajectories.

</details>


### [85] [Multiple-group (Controlled) Interrupted Time Series Analysis with Higher-Order Autoregressive Errors: A Simulation Study Comparing Newey-West and Prais-Winsten Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24814)
*Ariel Linden*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 在多重分组控制中断时间序列分析中，当存在高阶自相关时，Prais-Winsten回归比OLS-Newey-West方法提供更可靠的推断，后者虽然显示更高统计功效但存在严重的I类错误膨胀和置信区间覆盖不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究比较了OLS-Newey-West和Prais-Winsten回归在一阶自相关误差下的表现，但高阶自相关（如AR2、AR3）下的性能尚不清楚。随着Prais-Winsten扩展到AR(k)过程，需要评估这两种方法在高阶自相关下的表现差异。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛模拟，使用包含4个控制单元的MG-ITSA模型。数据在AR2和AR3误差结构下生成，涵盖轻度、振荡性和高度持续性自相关模式，时间序列长度从10到100个时间点，效应大小可变。治疗效果定义为水平和趋势的双重差分。评估指标包括统计功效、95%置信区间覆盖率、I类错误率、百分比偏差、均方根误差和经验标准误。进行敏感性分析检验替代设计。

Result: 两种方法均产生近似无偏估计。OLS-NW显示更高统计功效，但存在I类错误膨胀和覆盖率不足问题，特别是在高阶自相关和较长序列时。在高度持续性自相关下，OLS-NW在100个时间点的覆盖率降至45-50%，而PW保持91-94%的覆盖率。OLS-NW的I类错误率上升至50-57%。PW在某些设置下显示出统计功效优势。

Conclusion: 在一阶自相关误差下观察到的统计功效与有效推断之间的权衡在高阶自相关下更加恶化。OLS-NW的明显功效优势反映了虚假阳性的膨胀。Prais-Winsten提供更可靠的推断，在假设检验和误差控制方面更受推荐。

Abstract: Background: Multiple group controlled interrupted time series analysis (MG-ITSA) is widely used to evaluate healthcare interventions. Prior studies compared ordinary least squares with Newey-West standard errors (OLS-NW) and Prais-Winsten (PW) regression under first order autoregressive (AR1) errors, but performance under higher order autocorrelation is unclear. Recent extensions of PW to AR(k) processes allow such comparisons.
  Methods: We conducted a Monte Carlo simulation using an MG-ITSA model with four control units. Data were generated under AR2 and AR3 error structures representing mild, oscillatory, and highly persistent autocorrelation across series lengths from 10 to 100 time points and varying effect sizes. Treatment effects were defined as difference in differences in level and trend. We evaluated power, 95 percent confidence interval coverage, type I error, percent bias, root mean squared error, and empirical standard errors. Sensitivity analyses examined alternative designs.
  Results: Both methods produced approximately unbiased estimates. OLS-NW showed higher power but inflated type I error and poor coverage, especially with higher AR order and longer series. Under highly persistent autocorrelation, OLS-NW coverage fell to 45 to 50 percent at 100 time points, while PW maintained 91 to 94 percent coverage. Type I error for OLS-NW rose to 50 to 57 percent. PW showed power advantages in some settings.
  Conclusions: The tradeoff between power and valid inference seen under AR1 errors worsens with higher order autocorrelation. The apparent power advantage of OLS-NW reflects inflated false positives. PW provides more reliable inference and is preferred for hypothesis testing and error control.

</details>


### [86] [Bayesian Inference for Epidemic Final Size Datasets with Hidden Underlying Household Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25235)
*Joseph Brooks,Thomas House,Lorenzo Pellis,Joe Hilton*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出新的贝叶斯推断方法，通过MCMC算法推断传播强度，解决家庭二次发病率（SAR）因家庭规模分布不同而难以推广的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭二次发病率（SAR）作为传染病传播潜力的常用指标，依赖于研究期间的家庭规模分布，难以推广到新情境。需要能够估计人与人传播率的方法，以便与人口结构数据结合来参数化机制性传播模型

Method: 开发新的贝叶斯推断方法，使用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）算法，通过插补未报告的家庭结构来推断传播强度。该方法可处理不同分辨率水平的家庭流行病学数据

Result: 对于基于现实家庭规模分布的合成数据，能够持续实现超过95%的传播率估计覆盖率；对于病理性家庭规模分布的数据，在获得强信息的情况下也能达到类似覆盖率。使用COVID-19疫情中的实际数据集显示，按家庭规模分层观察的SAR能显著减少估计的不确定性

Conclusion: 进行家庭流行病学研究的研究人员可以通过报告家庭分层估计来提高结果对传染病建模者的实用性。这些结果鼓励在流行病学现场工作中报告更高分辨率的输出，因为在缺乏强先验信息的情况下，传播参数难以从通常报告的低分辨率数据集中识别

Abstract: Households represent a key unit of interest in infectious disease epidemiology, in both empirical studies and mathematical modelling. The within-household transmission potential of a disease is often summarised by a secondary attack ratio (SAR). Despite its widespread use, the SAR depends on the household size distribution (HHSD) seen during the study period, making it difficult to generalise to new contexts. Extending estimates of transmission potential to new populations instead requires estimates of person-to-person transmission rates which can be convoluted with data on population structure to parametrise mechanistic transmission models. In this study we present a new Bayesian inference method which uses an MCMC algorithm to infer the transmission intensity by imputing the unreported household structure underlying the epidemic. This method can be run on household epidemiological data reported at varying levels of resolution. For synthetic data from a realistic underlying HHSD, we were able to achieve over 95% coverage in our estimates of transmission rate consistently. We were also able to consistently achieve over 95% coverage for data generated with a pathological underlying HHSD, given strong information about the HHSD. Using an existing dataset which recorded micro-scale household epidemiological outcomes during the COVID-19 pandemic, we show that stratifying observed SARs by household size substantially reduces the uncertainty in estimates. Our findings suggest that researchers conducting household epidemiological studies can improve the utility of results for infectious disease modellers by reporting household-stratified estimates. These results aim to encourage the reporting of higher resolution outputs in epidemiological field work as, in the absence of strong priors, transmission parameters were not easily identifiable from low resolution datasets, which are often reported.

</details>


### [87] [A Bayesian Gamma-power-mixture survival regression model: predicting the recurrence of prostate cancer post-prostatectomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25455)
*Tommy Walker Mackay,Mingtong Xu,Shahrokh F. Shariat,Roger Sewell*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 使用贝叶斯Gamma-power-mixture生存回归模型分析423例局限性前列腺癌根治术患者数据，发现仅使用年龄和术前血液检测（PSA和生物标志物）即可获得显著的表观香农信息（ASI），优于其他临床变量组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估不同术前变量子集对前列腺癌根治术后生化复发时间的信息价值，比较血液生物标志物与其他临床变量（如Gleason分级、MRI结果）在预测复发时间方面的信息贡献。

Method: 使用贝叶斯Gamma-power-mixture生存回归模型，在423例局限性前列腺癌根治术患者数据集中，计算不同变量子集对生化复发时间的表观香农信息（ASI）。采用贪婪算法选择最佳生物标志物组合。

Result: 仅使用年龄和术前血液检测（PSA和生物标志物）获得0.232 nats（0.335 bits）ASI，显著优于先前研究结果。血液生物标志物（TGFβ1、VCAM1、IL6sR、uPA）的信息价值远高于Gleason分级或MRI结果。其他临床变量（术前/术后Gleason分级、手术发现、临床分期、前列腺外侵犯等）未能增加ASI。

Conclusion: 血液生物标志物在预测前列腺癌根治术后生化复发时间方面具有最高的信息价值，优于传统临床病理变量。这支持了血液生物标志物在前列腺癌预后评估中的重要作用。

Abstract: In a dataset of 423 patients who had had radical prostatectomy for localised prostate cancer we estimated the apparent Shannon information (ASI) about time to biochemical recurrence in various subsets of the available pre-op variables using a Bayesian Gamma-power-mixture survival regression model.
  In all the subsets examined the ASI was positive with posterior probability greater than 0.975 .
  Using only age and results of pre-operative blood tests (PSA and biomarkers) we achieved 0.232 (0.180 to 0.290) nats ASI (0.335 (0.260 to 0.419) bits) (posterior mean and equitailed 95% posterior confidence intervals). This is more than double the mean posterior ASI previously achieved on the same dataset by a subset of the current authors using a log-skew-Student-mixture model, and is greater than that previous value with posterior probability greater than 0.99 . Additionally using pre- or post-operative Gleason grades, operative findings, clinical stage, and presence or absence of extraprostatic extension or seminal vesicle invasion did not increase the ASI extracted. However removing the blood-based biomarkers and replacing them with either pre-operative Gleason grades or findings available from MRI scanning greatly reduced the available ASI to respectively 0.077 (0.038 to 0.120) and 0.088 (0.045 to 0.132) nats (both less than the values using blood-based biomarkers with posterior probability greater than 0.995). A greedy approach to selection of the best biomarkers gave TGFbeta1, VCAM1, IL6sR, and uPA in descending order of importance from those examined.

</details>

### [88] [Bound states of anyons: a geometric quantization approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24701)
*Qingchen Li,Pavel A. Nosov,Taige Wang,Eslam Khalaf*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种在任意子希尔伯特空间内研究任意子束缚的可控可扩展方法，应用于ν=1/3 Laughlin态准空穴，发现纯排斥相互作用下仍能形成束缚态，这是贝里相位效应驱动的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究任意子相互作用及其可能的束缚态对于理解分数量子霍尔态物理至关重要，需要一种在任意子希尔伯特空间内可控且可扩展的理论方法。

Method: 引入在任意子希尔伯特空间内工作的理论框架，包含表征经典任意子构型静电能的等效势和同时编码任意子贝里相位及其希尔伯特空间结构的Kähler势；应用Kähler流形上的几何量子化构造任意子哈密顿量；使用蒙特卡洛方法计算大系统并外推到热力学极限。

Result: 对于ν=1/3 Laughlin态准空穴，当屏蔽长度小于或等于磁长度时，Laughlin准空穴形成束缚态，尽管电子-电子相互作用和准空穴静电势都是纯排斥的；束缚态形成是贝里相位效应驱动的；随着屏蔽长度减小，观察到自由e/3任意子、配对2e/3束缚态、三任意子电荷-e团簇等相序列。

Conclusion: 提出了一种研究任意子束缚的通用理论框架，揭示了纯排斥相互作用下任意子束缚的可能性，这是贝里相位效应的结果；该方法可用于量子霍尔系统的电荷成像实验和分数量子反常霍尔材料中巡游任意子相的相图研究。

Abstract: The question of anyon interactions and their possible binding plays a key role in the physics of fractional quantum Hall states. Here, we introduce a controlled and scalable approach to study anyon binding by working entirely within the Hilbert space of anyons. The resulting theory is characterized by an effective potential, which captures the electrostatic energy of classical anyon configurations, and a Kähler potential, which simultaneously encodes the anyon Berry phase and the structure of their Hilbert space; both quantities are readily computed using Monte Carlo methods for large systems, enabling reliable extrapolation to the thermodynamic limit. By applying the formalism of geometric quantization on Kähler manifolds, we construct the anyon Hamiltonian, which can be exactly diagonalized in the few-anyon Hilbert space. Applying our approach to the quasiholes of the $ν=1/3$ Laughlin state with screened Coulomb interaction, we find that Laughlin quasiholes form bound states for screening lengths comparable or smaller than the magnetic length. Remarkably, binding occurs despite both the bare electron-electron interaction and the quasihole electrostatic potential being purely repulsive. The bound-state formation is a Berry phase effect, driven by oscillations in the quasihole density profile on the $\ell_B$ scale that are invisible in the quasihole electrostatic potential alone. For multiple quasiholes, we identify a sequence of phases as the screening length is reduced: free $e/3$ anyons, paired $2e/3$ bound states, three-anyon charge-$e$ clusters, and larger composite objects. Finally, we discuss possible signatures in charge imaging experiments on quantum Hall systems and the relevance to the phase diagram of itinerant anyon phases in fractional quantum anomalous Hall materials.

</details>


### [89] [Topological properties of gapless phases in an interacting spinful wire](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24831)
*Polina Matveeva,Dmitri Gutman,Sam T. Carr*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了具有自旋-电荷分离的相互作用自旋模型中无能隙相位的拓扑性质，重点关注了Z2对称性破缺相之间的边界，发现了两种拓扑非平庸的无能隙态：拓扑Luther-Emery液体和拓扑Mott绝缘体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索相互作用系统中无能隙相位的拓扑性质，特别是在自旋-电荷分离的背景下，理解Z2对称性破缺相边界处的拓扑特征。

Method: 研究聚焦于相互作用自旋模型中的无能隙边界，分析拓扑非平庸和拓扑平庸绝缘体边界处的物理状态。通过理论分析揭示了两种拓扑非平庸无能隙态的存在。

Result: 发现了两种拓扑非平庸的无能隙态：1) 拓扑Luther-Emery液体，具有无能隙电荷激发和携带分数自旋的拓扑边缘模式；2) 拓扑Mott绝缘体，具有无能隙自旋激发和携带分数电荷的边缘态。这些相互作用无能隙相可以绝热连接到非相互作用的拓扑金属。

Conclusion: 研究证明了即使在缺乏局域序参量的相互作用无能隙系统中，拓扑非平庸态仍然存在，并且可以绝热连接到非相互作用的拓扑金属，揭示了相互作用系统中拓扑相的新颖特征。

Abstract: We study topology in gapless phases of an interacting spinful model with spin-charge separation. We focus on the gapless boundaries between $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry-breaking phases. We find two topologically non-trivial gapless states that occur at the boundary between a non-trivial and a trivial insulator. They correspond to topological Luther-Emery liquid and topological Mott insulator. The Luther-Emery liquid is characterized by gapless charge excitations and features topological edge modes that carry fractional spin, while the topological Mott insulator has gapless spin sector and features edge states that carry fractional charge. Surprisingly, even though there is no mean-field description of the interacting gapless phases, as there is no local order parameter, we show that they can be adiabatically connected to a non-interacting topological metal. This non-interacting state is a phase boundary between decoupled Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chains with the winding number $ν=2$ and chains with $ν=1$.

</details>


### [90] [Dynamical Response of the Kitaev Spin Liquid under Third-Nearest-Neighbor Heisenberg Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24918)
*Chuan Chen,Jiucai Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究Kitaev自旋液体在第三近邻海森堡相互作用J3扰动下的动力学性质，发现J3诱导出类似顺磁子的集体模式，这些模式与高能Majorana连续谱共存，其软化预示着向磁有序的量子相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于越来越多的证据表明第三近邻海森堡相互作用J3在候选Kitaev材料中具有重要意义，需要研究Kitaev自旋液体在J3扰动下的动力学特性，包括自旋动力学结构因子和拉曼散射，以解释实验光谱。

Method: 采用自洽的部分子平均场加随机相位近似框架，研究Kitaev自旋液体在J3扰动下的动力学性质。通过自旋动力学结构因子分析集体模式，并计算拉曼散射响应，区分不同激发通道的特征。

Result: J3诱导出相干、类似顺磁子的集体模式，与高能Majorana连续谱共存。随着|J3|增加，这些模式软化，标志着向磁有序的量子相变。磁有序在铁磁和反铁磁Kitaev模型中具有相同的临界J3值，且通过四子格对偶变换形成精确的对偶对。外磁场进一步软化预存的顺磁子模式，增强磁有序。拉曼散射显示，Kitaev类顶点仅探测巡游物质Majorana费米子，而J3类顶点同时包含物质Majorana和vison激发。

Conclusion: 该研究为J3扰动的Kitaev自旋液体动力学特征提供了统一视角，有助于解释具有显著J3相互作用的候选Kitaev材料的实验光谱。J3诱导的集体模式及其软化行为是识别量子相变和磁有序的关键特征，而拉曼散射中的不同激发通道提供了区分物质Majorana和vison激发的有效手段。

Abstract: Motivated by growing evidence for the significance of the third-nearest-neighbor Heisenberg ($J_3$) interaction in candidate Kitaev materials, we investigate the dynamical properties of the Kitaev spin liquid (KSL) under a $J_3$ perturbation, focusing on its spin dynamical structure factor (DSF) and Raman scattering. Within a self-consistent parton mean-field plus random-phase approximation framework, we find that $J_3$ induces coherent, paramagnon-like collective modes that coexist with a high-energy Majorana continuum in the spin DSF. The softening of these modes with increasing $|J_3|$ signals a quantum phase transition to magnetic order. Remarkably, magnetic ordering sets in at a common critical $J_3$ for both ferromagnetic ($K<0$) and antiferromagnetic ($K>0$) Kitaev models, with the resulting ordered states forming exact dual pairs under a four-sublattice duality transformation that maps $(K,J_3) \rightarrow (-K,J_3)$. An external magnetic field further softens the preexisting paramagnon modes, thereby enhancing magnetic order. Perturbative Raman calculations show that while the Kitaev-like Raman vertex probes only itinerant matter Majorana fermions, the response from the $J_3$-like vertex features both matter Majoranas and visons. Four-vison excitations produce a sharp peak accompanied by a two-fermion continuum, whereas two-vison excitations yield a continuum closely resembling the single-matter-fermion density of states. These results provide a unified perspective on the dynamical signatures of $J_3$-perturbed KSL and are helpful for interpreting experimental spectra in candidate Kitaev materials with sizable $J_3$ interactions.

</details>


### [91] [Microscopic nature of $4a_0\times4a_0$ plaquettes in stripe LDOS and $2a_0$ shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25245)
*Ying Liang,Yi-Da Chu,Shi-Jie Hu,Xue-Feng Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过量子色弦模型框架揭示了欠掺杂铜氧化物中普遍存在的4a₀×4a₀晶格结构与自旋子单重态对之间的微观关联，并识别了粒子-空穴对称性破缺导致的2a₀偏移效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描隧道显微镜（STM）能够可视化单个条纹的局域态密度（LDOS），这对于理解欠掺杂铜氧化物中超导性与条纹结构之间的强关联至关重要。奇异的LDOS图模式是揭示潜在微观机制的关键，但需要新的理论框架来解释这些实验现象。

Method: 采用量子色弦模型框架进行分析，将理论计算结果与铜氧化物的实验LDOS数据进行对比。通过研究更长的条纹（L=18）来验证粒子-空穴对称性破缺效应。

Result: 研究发现：1）普遍存在的4a₀×4a₀晶格结构的微观起源与自旋子单重态对密切相关；2）识别出粒子-空穴对称性破缺效应，表现为2a₀的偏移，这一效应在更长的条纹（L=18）中得到确认。

Conclusion: 该工作为解释实验中的STM信号提供了基于波函数的新视角，可能推动对高温超导铜氧化物微观理解的进展。量子色弦模型框架能够有效关联微观机制与实验观测的LDOS模式。

Abstract: Scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) serves as a powerful pictorial tool for visualizing the local density of states (LDOS) of an individual stripe, which strongly intertwines with superconductivity in the underdoped cuprates. The exotic LDOS map patterns thus appear as the key to uncovering the mystery of the underlying microscopic mechanisms. With the quantum color string model framework, we reveal that the microscopic origin of the ubiquitous $4a_0\times4a_0$ plaquettes is closely related to spinon singlet pairs. Moreover, by comparing our data with LDOS of cuprates, we identify an effect of particle-hole symmetry breaking (PHSB): a $2a_0$ shift, which is confirmed in a longer stripe ($L=18$).Our work offers a fresh wavefunction-based perspective for interpreting STM signals in experiments and may advance the microscopic comprehension of high-$T_c$ cuprates.

</details>


### [92] [Structural and magnetic phases of topological kagome metal Fe$_3$Sn$_2$ under pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25271)
*S. Chattopadhyay,L. Thomarat,C. S. Ong,K. Kargeti,Lipika,J. -P. Rueff,L. Nataf,K. Manna,S. K. Panda,C. Shekhar,V. Balédent*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: Fe₃Sn₂在高压下发生结构相变和高自旋到低自旋转变，压力通过增强自旋轨道耦合和Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用重塑磁性能量景观，为调控kagome材料的磁各向异性和拓扑相提供新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究压力如何调控kagome铁磁体Fe₃Sn₂的晶体结构和磁性，探索压力作为工程化磁各向异性和潜在拓扑相的有效控制参数。

Method: 结合X射线衍射(XRD)、X射线发射光谱(XES)、X射线磁圆二色性(XMCD)实验技术，以及自旋极化密度泛函理论(DFT)计算，系统研究高压下Fe₃Sn₂的结构和磁性演化。

Result: 在~20 GPa以上发生结构相变，伴随Fe局域磁矩显著减小的高自旋到低自旋转变。压力-温度磁相图显示丰富的磁性行为：室温下c轴共线铁磁相持续到结构相变；低温下倾斜磁构型在更高压力下保持稳定；中间温度下压力稳定低温磁相。计算表明压力增强自旋轨道驱动的磁各向异性和Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用，有利于非共线磁构型。

Conclusion: 压力通过耦合晶格、自旋态和相对论性磁相互作用，重塑Fe₃Sn₂的磁性能量景观，确立静水压力作为调控kagome材料磁各向异性和潜在拓扑相的有效参数。

Abstract: We investigate the pressure-induced evolution of crystal structure and magnetism in the kagome ferromagnet Fe$_3$Sn$_2$ by combining X-ray diffraction, X-ray Emission Spectroscopy, X-ray Magnetic Circular Dichroism, and spin-polarized density functional theory calculations. X-ray diffraction reveals a structural phase transition above $\sim$20~GPa, which coincides with a pronounced reduction of the local Fe magnetic moment evidenced by X-ray emission spectroscopy, indicating a high-spin to low-spin transition. While XES probes the amplitude of the local moment, XMCD provides direct information on the orientation of the ordered magnetic moments and uncovers a rich pressure--temperature magnetic phase diagram. At room temperature, a collinear ferromagnetic phase with moments aligned along the $c$ axis persists up to the structural transition. At low temperature, a tilted magnetic configuration remains stable to significantly higher pressures, while at intermediate temperatures pressure stabilizes the low-temperature magnetic phase at the expense of the high-temperature one. Spin-polarized first-principles calculations show that, although isotropic ferromagnetic exchange interactions remain robust under compression, pressure enhances spin--orbit--driven magnetic anisotropy and Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interactions, favoring non-collinear magnetic configurations. Our results demonstrate that pressure reshapes the magnetic energy landscape of Fe$_3$Sn$_2$ by coupling lattice, spin state, and relativistic magnetic interactions, establishing hydrostatic pressure as an effective control parameter to engineer magnetic anisotropy and potentially topological phases in kagome materials.

</details>


### [93] [Flat band driven competing charge and spin instabilities in the altermagnet CrSb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25317)
*A. Korshunov,M. Alkorta,C. -Y. Lim,F. Ballester,Cong Li,Zhilin Li,D. Chernyshov,A. Bosak,M. G. Vergniory,Ion Errea,S. Blanco-Canosa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: CrSb中平坦能带物理导致电荷序与自旋序竞争，在TN处产生巨大自旋-声子耦合和Kohn异常


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动量空间电子波函数局域化产生的平坦能带如何增强相互作用效应，以及电荷、自旋和晶格自由度之间的强纠缠如何导致竞争序参量和集体行为

Method: 实验观测CrSb中布里渊区M点(q*=(1/2 0))的短程电荷序涨落，测量声子色散在TN处的Kohn异常，结合第一性原理计算分析平坦电子态与声子分支的耦合

Result: 发现TN以上存在短程电荷序涨落，进入磁有序相后消失；TN处q*点声子色散出现明显Kohn异常；TN以下交换伸缩效应使软声子模重整化约6 meV，为迄今报道的最大自旋-声子耦合

Conclusion: 平坦能带物理放大的电荷与自旋序参量竞争驱动了观测到的声子异常及其在TN处的突变重构，CrSb成为研究平坦能带如何介导巨大自旋-声子耦合和竞争对称破缺的理想平台

Abstract: The confinement of electronic wavefunctions in momentum space can give rise to flat electronic bands, where the quenching of kinetic energy enhances the density of states and amplifies interaction effects. Such conditions are fertile ground for emergent quantum phases, as spin, charge and lattice degrees of freedom become strongly entangled. In these regimes, subtle competitions between intertwined order parameters often dictate the macroscopic ground state, producing complex and sometimes unexpected collective behavior. Here we show that the altermagnet CrSb provides a realization of this scenario, and uncover short-range charge-order fluctuations at the M point of the Brillouin zone, q*=(1/2 0), persisting above the Neel temperature (TN). Remarkably, these fluctuations collapse upon entering the magnetically ordered phase, revealing a direct and robust competition between charge and spin order. At TN, the phonon dispersion at q* develops a pronounced Kohn-like anomaly, signaling strong electron-phonon coupling in the vicinity of the magnetic transition. Below TN, exchange striction dramatically renormalizes the associated soft phonon mode by approximately ~6 meV, the largest spin-phonon coupling ever reported. First-principles calculations attribute this behavior to a strong coupling between nearly dispersionless electronic states and a phonon branch that appears unstable at the harmonic level only when no magnetic order is considered, revealing the large sensitivity of the lattice to magnetic symmetry breaking. The competition between charge and spin order parameters, amplified by flat-band physics, drives the observed phonon anomaly and its abrupt reconstruction at TN. With its chemically simple structure and symmetry-protected altermagnetic state, CrSb emerges as a model platform to explore how flat electronic bands mediate giant spin-phonon coupling and competing broken symmetries.

</details>


### [94] [Stabilization of zigzag order in NiPS$_3$ via positive biquadratic interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25475)
*Qiang Luo,Shuhang Yang,Xiaoying Wang,Zhengyu Jiang,Chunlan Ma,Yan Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过DFT计算和理论分析，确定了NiPS₃中双二次交换相互作用B/J₃≈0.44，支持了zigzag基态有序


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管对NiPS₃的自旋哈密顿量已有广泛研究，但关于其精确形式仍存在争议，特别是双二次交换项B的大小和作用尚不明确

Method: 采用密度泛函理论计算提取相互作用参数，结合最小增强自旋波理论和密度矩阵重整化群计算验证参数合理性

Result: 获得正的双二次相互作用B/J₃≈0.44，在J₁-J₃-B模型中这些参数能自然稳定zigzag有序，DMRG计算进一步验证了参数对基态描述的合理性

Conclusion: 虽然完全确定NiPS₃的自旋哈密顿量仍需进一步研究，但本文结果为理解其双二次相互作用提供了新见解

Abstract: Despite extensive research, the precise spin Hamiltonian of the van der Waals antiferromagnet NiPS$_3$ -- which hosts a zigzag-ordered ground state -- remains debated. While consensus has emerged on ferromagnetic nearest-neighbor ($J_1$) and antiferromagnetic third-nearest-neighbor ($J_3$) Heisenberg interactions, recent studies suggest a biquadratic ($B$) exchange term may also play a role, though its estimated magnitude varies widely. To address this controversy, we perform density functional theory calculations and extract a positive biquadratic interaction with $B/J_3 \approx 0.44$. Within the minimal $J_1$-$J_3$-$B$ model, we show that these parameters naturally stabilize zigzag ordering using minimally augmented spin-wave theory. Density-matrix renormalization group calculations further validate our extracted parameters as a reasonable description of the ground state. Although fully resolving the spin Hamiltonian of NiPS$_3$ requires further investigation, our findings provide new insights into its biquadratic interaction.

</details>


### [95] [Majorana-assisted nonlocal spin correlation in quasi-one-dimensional Kitaev spin liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25647)
*Yuki Yamazaki,Shingo Kobayashi,Akira Furusaki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种通过Majorana零模实现非局域自旋关联的方法，作为Kitaev自旋液体中Majorana非局域性的具体表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Kitaev自旋液体中Majorana非局域性的具体物理表现，寻找能够实验观测的Majorana零模特征信号。

Method: 在准一维Kitaev蜂窝模型的零通量扇区中，分析其拓扑性质，发现两端局域的Majorana零模。将非局域Majorana费米子宇称算子映射到非局域自旋弦算子上，通过局部微扰消除冗余基态简并度，同时保持Majorana和总费米子宇称。

Result: 数值计算确认了由Majorana零模产生的有限非局域自旋关联，且没有任何局域磁化。非局域自旋关联与Majorana宇称算子和总费米子宇称算子的乘积成正比。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了量子自旋液体中内在Majorana非局域性的具体特征，为实验探测Majorana零模提供了新的物理信号。

Abstract: We propose Majorana-assisted nonlocal spin correlation as a manifestation of Majorana nonlocality in quasi-one-dimensional (1D) Kitaev spin liquids. Focusing on the flux-free sector of the Kitaev honeycomb model in a quasi-1D geometry, we uncover its topological nature and show that it hosts Majorana zero modes localized at both ends, which are stabilized by finite-size-induced topology. We further show that the nonlocal Majorana fermion parity operator, $P_{\text{MF}}=iγ_{\text{L}}γ_{\text{R}}$, is mapped to a nonlocal spin-string operator, producing an end-to-end spin correlation proportional to the product of $P_{\text{MF}}$ and total fermion parity operators when local perturbations remove redundant ground-state degeneracies while preserving the Majorana and total fermion parities in the flux-free sector. Numerical calculations confirm a finite nonlocal spin correlation generated by these Majorana zero modes without any local magnetization. Our results establish a concrete signature of intrinsic Majorana nonlocality in quantum spin liquids.

</details>

### [96] [The Spatial Hydrodynamic Attractor: Resurgence of the Gradient Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24611)
*Mahdi Kooshkbaghi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 证明了非相对论空间梯度级数虽然阶乘发散但严格Borel可和，而相对论因果性约束则使空间流体力学展开收敛且有有限收敛半径


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究远离平衡态动力学系统坍缩到流体力学吸引子的过程中，空间梯度展开的解析结构问题。传统的时间梯度级数是非Borel可和的，需要transseries补全，但空间展开的解析结构一直不清楚。

Method: 通过Lagrange反演推导了所有阶的精确闭式Chapman-Enskog系数，分析了非相对论和相对论情况下的空间梯度级数性质

Result: 1. 非相对论空间梯度级数虽然阶乘发散，但严格Borel可和；2. 这种发散源于无界的伽利略速度；3. 施加相对论因果性约束后，空间流体力学展开收敛且有有限收敛半径

Conclusion: 空间梯度展开的收敛性取决于物理约束：非相对论情况下级数发散但Borel可和，而相对论因果性确保了空间流体力学展开的收敛性，这为流体力学吸引子的数学描述提供了重要理论依据

Abstract: Far-from-equilibrium kinetic systems collapse onto a hydrodynamic attractor, traditionally approximated by a gradient expansion. While temporal gradient series are non-Borel summable and require transseries completions, the analytic structure of the spatial expansion has remained elusive. Here, we derive exact closed-form Chapman--Enskog coefficients at all orders via Lagrange inversion and prove that the non-relativistic spatial gradient series, though factorially divergent, is strictly Borel summable. Furthermore, we show that this divergence originates from unbounded Galilean velocities; enforcing relativistic causality yields a convergent spatial hydrodynamic expansion with finite radius.

</details>


### [97] [The Dynamic Doppler Spectrum Induced by Nonlinear Sensor Motion: Relativistic Kinematics and 4D Frenet-Serret Spacetime Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24870)
*Bryce M. Barclay,Alex Mahalov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文分析了非惯性接收器观测到的电磁信号，使用两种框架描述相对论运动：高阶运动学4向量（加速度和急动度）和4D Frenet-Serret框架（曲率和挠率），推导了动态多普勒效应的紧凑表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性相对论运动分析的核心在于精确表征诱导的动态多普勒效应。研究旨在为非惯性接收器观测的电磁信号提供理论框架，为雷达、传感和通信系统等工程应用提供诊断和预测工具。

Method: 采用两种框架描述相对论运动：1）使用高阶运动学4向量（相对论加速度和急动度）描述观测者路径，推导谱变换的紧凑表达式；2）使用4D Frenet-Serret框架和观测者路径的曲率、挠率等几何参数描述信号幅度和相位波动。

Result: 相对论加速度导致指数谱展宽和指数幅度增长/衰减；急动度在观测信号中诱导非线性偏斜啁啾；曲率和挠率等几何参数提供了信号幅度和相位波动的简洁可解释描述。

Conclusion: 通过两种框架获得了非惯性动态多普勒效应的简洁、可解释描述，为工程应用（包括雷达、传感和通信系统）提供了有用的诊断和预测工具。

Abstract: Fundamental to the analysis of nonlinear relativistic motion is the precise characterization of the induced dynamic Doppler effects. In this work, we analyze the electromagnetic signals observed by non-inertial receivers using two frameworks to describe the relativistic motion. We first consider observer paths described by higher-order kinematic 4 vectors: relativistic acceleration and jolt. The dynamic Doppler effects of relativistic acceleration and jolt are exponential spectral broadening and exponential amplitude growth or decay. We derive compact expressions for the spectrum transformation resulting from relativistic acceleration and jolt. The jolt induces nonlinear skewed chirps in observed signals. Next we consider observer paths described by the 4D Frenet-Serret frame and the curvature and torsion of the observer path. We obtain descriptions of the amplitude and phase fluctuations of the signal in terms of the geometric parameters of curvature and torsion. Concise, interpretable descriptions of non-inertial dynamic Doppler effects provide a useful diagnostic and predictive tool for engineering applications including radar, sensing, and communications systems.

</details>


### [98] [KdV integrability in GUE correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24956)
*Di Yang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文基于Okounkov公式和GUE关联函数与Toda格层次结构的关系，为Witten-Kontsevich定理提供了新证明，该定理将Witten相交数与KdV可积层次结构联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Witten-Kontsevich定理是数学物理中的重要结果，将拓扑弦论中的相交数与可积系统联系起来。已有多种证明方法，但本文旨在基于Okounkov的GUE关联函数公式和Toda格层次结构的知识，提供一个新的、更简洁的证明途径。

Method: 利用Okounkov公式将n点GUE关联函数与相同亏格的Witten相交数联系起来，同时利用GUE关联函数的配分函数是Toda格层次结构的tau函数这一事实。通过分析这两个数学结构之间的关系，推导出Witten相交数与KdV层次结构的对应关系。

Result: 成功给出了Witten-Kontsevich定理的新证明，展示了如何从GUE关联函数和Toda格层次结构出发，通过数学推导得到Witten相交数与KdV可积层次结构的等价性。

Conclusion: 本文通过连接随机矩阵理论（GUE）、可积系统（Toda格和KdV层次）和拓扑弦论（Witten相交数），为Witten-Kontsevich定理提供了一个优雅的新证明，揭示了这些看似不同数学领域之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: Okounkov [36] proved a remarkable formula relating $n$-point GUE (Gaussian unitary ensemble) correlators of a fixed genus to Witten's intersection numbers of the same genus. The partition function of GUE correlators is a tau-function for the Toda lattice hierarchy. In this note, based on the knowledge of these two statements we give a new proof of the Witten--Kontsevich theorem, that relates Witten's intersection numbers to the KdV (Korteweg--de Vries) integrable hierarchy.

</details>


### [99] [A Concentration of Measure Phenomenon in Lattice Yang-Mills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25236)
*Tamer Tlas*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文证明Yang-Mills作用的Haar测度乘积的推前映射收敛于高斯分布，并概述如何由此恢复强耦合展开


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Yang-Mills理论中Haar测度乘积在Yang-Mills作用下的统计行为，为理解格点规范理论的强耦合展开提供数学基础

Method: 通过分析Yang-Mills作用对Haar测度乘积的推前映射，证明其收敛于高斯分布，并利用这一结果推导强耦合展开

Result: 证明了Yang-Mills作用的Haar测度乘积推前映射收敛为高斯分布，为强耦合展开提供了严格的数学基础

Conclusion: Yang-Mills作用的Haar测度乘积推前映射的高斯收敛性为格点规范理论的强耦合展开提供了严格的数学框架

Abstract: We demonstrate that the pushforward of the product of Haar measures by the lattice Yang-Mills action concentrates as a Gaussian. It is also sketched how, using this fact, one can recover the strong-coupling expansion.

</details>


### [100] [Chern-Simons theory in mathematics, condensed matter theory and cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25346)
*Jürg Fröhlich*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 综述Chern-Simons理论在代数拓扑、纽结理论、凝聚态物理和宇宙学中的应用，特别关注量子霍尔效应中的Chern-Simons作用，并描述五维阿贝尔Chern-Simons理论可能解释星际磁场的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统梳理Chern-Simons理论在不同领域的应用，特别是其在量子霍尔效应和宇宙学中的重要作用，为理解这一数学物理工具的多学科价值提供全面视角。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，分析Chern-Simons理论在代数拓扑、纽结理论、凝聚态物理和宇宙学中的具体应用案例，特别关注其在量子霍尔效应理论中的表现形式，并探讨五维阿贝尔Chern-Simons理论解释星际磁场起源的机制。

Result: Chern-Simons理论在多个领域展现出重要应用价值：在代数拓扑和纽结理论中提供数学框架；在凝聚态物理中成功描述整数和分数量子霍尔效应；在宇宙学中，五维阿贝尔Chern-Simons理论为观测到的星际磁场提供可能的起源机制。

Conclusion: Chern-Simons理论作为连接数学和物理的重要桥梁，在多个学科领域具有广泛而深刻的应用价值，特别是在量子霍尔效应和宇宙学中的成功应用，展示了这一理论框架的强大解释力和预测能力。

Abstract: Various applications of Chern-Simons theory in algebraic topology, in particular knot theory, condensed matter physics and cosmology are reviewed. Special attention is paid to appearances of Chern-Simons actions in the theory of the (integer and fractional) quantum Hall effect. A mechanism related to five-dimensional abelian Chern-Simons theory that may be at the origin of the observed intergalactic magnetic fields in the universe is described.

</details>


### [101] [On the integrability structure of the deformed rule-54 reversible cellular automaton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25424)
*Chiara Paletta,Tomaž Prosen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了规则-54可逆元胞自动机(RCA54)的量子变形和随机变形，分别在量子电路和马尔可夫链电路中分析了其可积结构，并提出了检测可积性的数字复杂度判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究RCA54模型在不同变形下的可积性结构，探索量子变形和随机变形两种不同物理设置中的守恒律和精确可解性，为理解可积系统的普适特征提供新视角。

Method: 1. 对于量子变形：构造范围-6的Lax算子，证明存在无限塔的相互对易守恒荷，通过交织算子证明转移矩阵与离散时间演化算子对易。
2. 对于随机变形：在开放边界条件下，采用交错块矩阵拟设（结合交换块态拟设和矩阵乘积态拟设）显式构造非平衡稳态。
3. 提出数字复杂度作为检测模型可积性或精确可解性的经验判据。

Result: 1. 量子变形中，最短非平凡守恒荷的密度支撑在六个连续格点上，属于由转移矩阵高阶对数导数生成的无限对易守恒荷塔。
2. 随机变形中，成功构造了开放边界条件下的非平衡稳态。
3. 提出了数字复杂度作为可积性检测的有效判据。

Conclusion: 论文系统研究了RCA54模型在量子变形和随机变形下的可积结构，证明了两种变形都保持可积性特征，并提出了检测可积性的新判据，为可积系统的研究提供了新方法和见解。

Abstract: We study quantum and stochastic deformations of the rule-54 reversible cellular automaton (RCA54) on a 1+1-dimensional spatiotemporal lattice, focusing on their integrability structures in two distinct settings. First, for the quantum deformation, which turns the model into an interaction-round-a-face brickwork quantum circuit (either on an infinite lattice or with periodic boundary conditions), we show that the shortest-range nontrivial conserved charge commuting with the discrete-time evolution operator has a density supported on six consecutive sites. By constructing the corresponding range-6 Lax operator, we prove that this charge belongs to an infinite tower of mutually commuting conserved charges generated by higher-order logarithmic derivatives of the transfer matrix. With the aid of an intertwining operator, we further prove that the transfer matrix commutes with the discrete-time evolution operator.
  Second, for the stochastic deformation, which renders the model into a Markov-chain circuit, we investigate open boundary conditions that couple the system at its edges to stochastic reservoirs. In this setting, we explicitly construct the non-equilibrium steady state (NESS) by means of a staggered patch matrix ansatz, a hybrid construction combining the previously used commutative patch-state ansatz for the undeformed RCA54 with the matrix-product ansatz. Finally, we propose a simple empirical criterion for detecting integrability or exact solvability in a given model setup, introducing the notion of digit complexity.

</details>


### [102] [The Maxwell class exact solutions to the Schrödinger equation and continuum mechanics models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25433)
*E. E. Perepelkin,B. I. Sadovnikov,N. G. Inozemtseva,A. S. Medvedev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 通过非线性勒让德变换应用于连续性方程，推导出薛定谔方程和连续介质力学方程的精确解，使用广义麦克斯韦分布作为动量密度函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在通过数学变换方法为薛定谔方程和连续介质力学方程寻找精确解析解，建立量子力学与经典连续介质力学之间的数学联系。

Method: 应用非线性勒让德变换处理连续性方程，采用广义麦克斯韦分布作为动量密度函数，推导出精确解并分析其数学物理特性。

Result: 获得了时间无关流动的矢量场、密度分布、量子势和经典势的显式表达式，并对结果进行了详细的数学和物理分析。

Conclusion: 通过非线性勒让德变换方法成功建立了薛定谔方程与连续介质力学方程之间的精确解对应关系，为理解量子与经典系统提供了新的数学框架。

Abstract: By applying the nonlinear Legendre transform to the continuity equation, this paper derives exact solutions to the Schrödinger equation and the equations of continuum mechanics. A generalized Maxwell distribution has been used as the momentum density function. Explicit expressions for the vector fields of time independent flows, density distributions, quantum and classical potentials have been found, and a detailed mathematical and physical analysis of the results obtained has been carried out.

</details>


### [103] [New convergence bound for the cluster expansion in canonical ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25592)
*Giuseppe Scola*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种新的聚合物活度选择方法，改进了正则系综中周期边界条件下簇展开的收敛界，并与已知结果进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统簇展开方法在正则系综中使用的聚合物活度选择可能不是最优的，作者希望找到更好的活度选择来改进收敛界，同时能够恢复热力学自由能的不可约Mayer系数。

Method: 在具有周期边界条件的正则系综中执行簇展开，引入了一种新的聚合物活度选择方法，该方法不同于标准选择。通过这种新方法推导改进的收敛界。

Result: 获得了比已知结果更好的簇展开收敛界，并且能够恢复热力学自由能的不可约Mayer系数。该方法还可应用于零边界条件和关联展开的收敛性分析。

Conclusion: 新的聚合物活度选择方法显著改进了正则系综中簇展开的收敛界，为统计力学中的展开方法提供了更优的理论框架，具有更广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: We perform a cluster expansion in the canonical ensemble with periodic boundary conditions, introducing a new choice of polymer activities that differs from the standard ones. This choice leads to an improved bound for the convergence of the cluster expansion, which we compare with the known one. We also recover the irreducible Mayer coefficients for the thermodynamic free energy. The results presented here can also be applied to the case of zero boundary conditions and to the convergence of correlation expansions.

</details>


### [104] [Critical curve of two-matrix models $ABBA$, $A\{B,A\}B$ and $ABAB$, Part I: Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25715)
*Carlos I. Pérez Sánchez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了一族双矩阵模型，通过蒙特卡洛方法估计了在(h,g)平面上最大收敛域的边界，并与精确解和功能重整化群获得的相图进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双矩阵模型在参数空间中的收敛性质，特别是最大收敛域的边界，这对于理解矩阵模型的相结构和临界行为具有重要意义。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛模拟方法，对三种不同相互作用项的双矩阵模型进行数值研究，估计其在(h,g)平面上的最大收敛域边界。

Result: 获得了每种情况下最大收敛域边界的蒙特卡洛估计值，并与已知的精确解（在唯一解析求解的情况下一致）以及通过功能重整化群获得的相图进行比较。

Conclusion: 蒙特卡洛方法能够有效估计双矩阵模型的收敛域边界，结果与理论预期一致，为理解这类模型的相结构提供了数值证据。

Abstract: For a family of two-matrix models \[ \frac{1}{2} \operatorname{Tr}(A^2+B^2) - \frac{g}{4} \operatorname{Tr}(A^4+B^4) - \begin{cases} \frac{h}{2} \operatorname{Tr}( A BA B) \\ \frac{h}{4} \operatorname{Tr}( A BA B+ ABBA ) \\ \frac{h}{2} \operatorname{Tr}( A B BA )
  \end{cases}
  \] with hermitian $A$ and $B$, we provide, in each case, a Monte Carlo estimate of the boundary of the maximal convergence domain in the $(h,g)$-plane. The results are discussed comparing with exact solutions (in agreement with the only analytically solved case) and phase diagrams obtained by means of the functional renormalization group.

</details>

### [105] [Diffractive and photon-induced processes at the LHC: from the odderon discovery, the evidence for saturation to the search for axion-like particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24808)
*C. Royon*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文讨论了三个主要研究领域：1) TOTEM和D0合作组发现的odderon；2) 喷注间隙测量对高胶子密度区域的敏感性及Pb-Pb相互作用中可能观测到的饱和现象；3) 对超出标准模型物理和通过光子-光子相互作用产生类轴子粒子的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在综述高能物理实验中的前沿发现和潜在新物理信号，包括强相互作用中的奇特交换粒子、高能核碰撞中的饱和现象，以及超出标准模型的新物理探索。

Method: 采用实验数据分析和理论解释相结合的方法，基于TOTEM、D0等实验合作组的测量结果，通过喷注间隙、核-核碰撞等观测手段，探索强相互作用、量子色动力学饱和现象以及新物理信号。

Result: 1) 确认了odderon的存在；2) 喷注间隙测量揭示了高胶子密度区域的特征；3) Pb-Pb相互作用中观测到可能的饱和现象迹象；4) 建立了对超出标准模型物理和类轴子粒子产生的敏感性。

Conclusion: 高能物理实验在多个前沿领域取得了重要进展，包括确认了odderon的存在、探索了量子色动力学饱和现象，并为超出标准模型物理的搜索提供了新的实验窗口，这些发现对理解强相互作用和探索新物理具有重要意义。

Abstract: We discuss first the discovery of the odderon by the TOTEM and D0 collaborations. We then describe the gap between jets measurements sensitive to the high gluon density regime and the possible observation of saturation phenomenon in Pb Pb interactions. We also mention the sensitivity to beyond standard model physics and to the production of axion-like particles via photon photon interactions.

</details>


### [106] [Further search for magnetic-field-induced neutron disappearance in an ultracold neutron beam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25376)
*Gaby Brenot,Benoit Clément,Hanno Filter-Pieler,Daniel Galbinski,Tobias Jenke,Thomas Lefort,Anthony Lejuez,Guillaume Pignol,Stephanie Roccia,William Saenz-Arevalo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 在ILL的PF2设施上进行的第二次中子-隐藏中子振荡实验，通过测量不同磁场下中子消失来探测振荡，未发现振荡证据并设定了振荡周期的下限


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究中子与隐藏中子之间的振荡现象，这种振荡可能揭示超出标准模型的新物理，特别是暗物质候选粒子

Method: 在PF2设施使用超冷中子束，通过改变外加磁场来共振增强不同质量分裂(δm)下的振荡概率，测量中子消失作为振荡证据

Result: 未观测到中子消失的证据，在95%置信水平下设定了中子-隐藏中子振荡周期的保守下限：对于|δm|∈[60,400]peV，τ_{nn'}>200ms；对于|δm|∈[400,1550]peV，τ_{nn'}>100ms

Conclusion: 实验未发现中子-隐藏中子振荡的证据，为这一现象设定了更严格的限制，排除了特定参数范围内的振荡可能性

Abstract: We report the results of the second iteration of an experiment searching for neutron-hidden-neutron oscillations in a beam of ultracold neutrons, conducted at the PF2 facility of the Institut Laue Langevin (ILL). Oscillations were tested via neutron disappearance as a function of an applied magnetic field, in the context of a phenomenological two-parameter model assuming zero hidden potentials. The magnetic field was varied in a step-wise manner in order to resonantly enhance the oscillation probability at different mass splittings ($δm$) across a 60--1550 peV range. No evidence for neutron disappearance is observed and conservative limits on the neutron-hidden-neutron oscillation period ($τ_{nn'}$) have been set at 95 % confidence level: $τ_{nn'} > 200$ms for $|δm| \in [60, 400]$ peV and $τ_{nn'} > 100$ ms for $|δm| \in [400, 1550]$ peV

</details>


### [107] [Measurement of dijet angular distributions and search for beyond the standard model physics in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25458)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS实验在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中测量了双喷注角分布，首次与NNLO QCD+EW理论预测比较，发现小差异，并设置了多种新物理场景的严格限制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高能对撞中的双喷注角分布，检验微扰QCD理论预测，并搜索超出标准模型的新物理现象，如夸克复合性、额外维度、量子黑洞、暗物质媒介等

Method: 使用CMS探测器在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中收集138 fb⁻¹数据，测量双喷注角分布，进行探测器效应修正，并与NNLO QCD（包含NLO电弱修正）理论预测比较

Result: 数据与理论预测基本一致，但在2.4-4.8 TeV和>6 TeV的双喷注质量区间观察到归一化分布形状的小差异；为多种新物理场景设定了迄今最严格的限制

Conclusion: 双喷注角分布测量为检验QCD理论提供了重要测试，同时为多种超出标准模型的新物理场景设定了严格限制，推动了高能物理前沿研究

Abstract: A measurement is presented of dijet angular distributions in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, using data collected with the CMS detector at the CERN LHC and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. For the first time, the dijet angular distributions, corrected for detector effects, are compared with the predictions of perturbative quantum chromodynamics at next-to-next-to-leading order, including next-to-leading-order electroweak corrections. While data are generally found to be in agreement with predictions, a small difference in shape of the normalized distributions is seen for dijet masses ranging from 2.4 to 4.8 TeV and above 6 TeV. The distributions are used to search for proposed signatures of quark compositeness, extra spatial dimensions, quantum black holes, dark-matter mediators, axion-like particles, and anomalous gluon couplings. The most stringent limits to date are set for most of these scenarios. Quark contact interactions are excluded at 95% confidence level (CL) up to a scale of 17 (37) TeV for destructive (constructive) interference in a benchmark scenario, valid to next-to-leading order in quantum chromodynamics, and in which only left-handed quarks participate. The coupling of axion-like particles to the gluon, $c_{\text{g}}/f_{\text{a}}$, is constrained to be lower than 0.42 TeV$^{-1}$ at 95% CL. The anomalous triple-gluon coupling, $C_{\text{G}}/Λ^2$, in a standard model effective field theory is constrained to be lower than 0.0076 TeV$^{-2}$ at 95% CL.

</details>


### [108] [Amplitude analysis and branching fraction measurement of the decay $D^0 \to K^+K^-π^0π^0$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25649)
*BESIII Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 首次对D^0→K^+K^-π^0π^0进行振幅分析，测量其绝对分支比，发现主要中间过程为D^0→K*(892)^+K*(892)^-，且该衰变为S波主导，纵向极化分数为0.468±0.046±0.011。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单卡比博压低的D^0→K^+K^-π^0π^0衰变过程，首次进行振幅分析以确定不同中间过程的相对幅度和相位，这对于理解粲介子衰变机制和强相互作用动力学具有重要意义。

Method: 使用BESIII探测器在质心能量3.773 GeV收集的e^+e^-对撞数据，对应积分亮度20.3 fb^{-1}。通过振幅分析方法确定各中间过程的相对贡献，测量绝对分支比和纵向极化分数。

Result: D^0→K^+K^-π^0π^0的绝对分支比已测量。主要中间过程为D^0→K*(892)^+K*(892)^-，分支比为(2.79±0.13±0.11)×10^{-3}。振幅分析显示该衰变为S波主导，纵向极化分数为0.468±0.046±0.011。

Conclusion: 首次对D^0→K^+K^-π^0π^0衰变进行了完整的振幅分析，确定了主要中间过程及其特性，为理解粲介子衰变中的强相互作用提供了重要实验依据。

Abstract: An amplitude analysis of the singly Cabibbo-suppressed decay $D^0 \to K^+ K^- π^0 π^0$ is performed, for the first time, to determine the relative magnitudes and phases of different intermediate processes. The analysis uses $e^+e^-$ collision data collected with the BESIII detector at the center-of-mass energy 3.773~GeV corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 20.3 $\rm fb^{-1}$. The absolute branching fraction of $D^0 \to K^+ K^- π^0 π^0$ is measured to be \BF. The dominant intermediate process is $D^0 \to K^{*}(892)^+K^{*}(892)^-$, with a branching fraction of $(2.79 \pm 0.13_{\rm{stat.}} \pm 0.11_{\rm{syst.}}) \times 10^{-3}$. Amplitude analysis reveals that the $D^0 \to K^{*}(892)^+K^{*}(892)^-$ decay is S-wave dominant. The longitudinal polarization fraction of $D^0 \to K^{*}(892)^+ K^{*}(892)^-$ is measured to be $0.468\pm0.046_{\rm{stat.}}\pm0.011_{\rm{syst.}}$.

</details>


### [109] [Worldwide Reactor Neutrino Propagation to Underground Labs: Matter Effects and Flux Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25655)
*Keyu Han,Juncheng Qian,Shaomin Chen*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文开发了一个高精度反应堆中微子通量预测框架，用于评估地球物质效应对地下实验室反应堆中微子测量的影响，以提高地球中微子实验的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球中微子作为地球物理研究的独特探针，可以揭示地球内部热源分布，但商业核电站产生的电子反中微子构成了难以消除的主要背景。随着地球中微子测量和反应堆背景建模接近亚百分之一精度，即使是微小的物质诱导修正也需要定量评估。

Method: 开发了高精度反应堆中微子通量预测框架，使用全球反应堆运行数据、反应堆-探测器距离以及中微子在地球中传播的物质效应（MSW）。为了高效求解三味MSW演化，在真空质量基中实现了二阶Strang分裂求解器。

Result: 计算了包含MSW效应的反应堆中微子振荡概率，比较了一维（球对称）和三维（包含横向不均匀性）地球模型下的结果与真空振荡情景，评估了地球结构特征对反应堆中微子通量预测精度的影响。

Conclusion: 该框架为高精度地球中微子实验提供了重要的背景修正工具，通过量化地球物质效应对反应堆中微子传播的影响，有助于提高地球中微子测量的准确性，从而更精确地揭示地球内部热源分布。

Abstract: As a unique probe for geophysical research, geoneutrinos can reveal the distribution of internal heat sources in the Earth by detecting electron antineutrinos produced by the radioactive decay of $^{238}$U, $^{232}$Th, and $^{40}$K. However, commercial nuclear power plants continuously produce the same type of electron antineutrinos, which constitute a primary background difficult to eliminate in geoneutrino experiments. As geoneutrino measurements and reactor background modeling approach sub-percent precision, even small matter-induced corrections to reactor antineutrino propagation require quantitative assessment. In this paper, we develop a high-precision prediction framework for reactor neutrino fluxes at underground labs, using global reactor operating data, reactor-to-detector distances, and matter effects (MSW) on neutrino propagation through the Earth. To solve the three-flavor MSW evolution efficiently, we implement a second-order Strang-splitting solver in the vacuum mass basis. Within this framework, we have calculated the reactor neutrino oscillation probabilities, including the MSW effect under one-dimensional (spherically symmetric) and three-dimensional (including lateral inhomogeneities) Earth models, and compared them with the vacuum oscillation scenario, to assess the impact of Earth's structural features on the accuracy of reactor neutrino flux predictions.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>
